# **AMENDED AND RESTATED TRANSITION SERVICES AGREEMENT** 

This AMENDED AND RESTATED TRANSITION SERVICES AGREEMENT (this “Agreement”), dated as of October 13, 2026, is entered into by and between Disney Worldwide Services, Inc., a Florida corporation (“Disney”), and Character Technologies, Inc., a Delaware corporation (“CAI”).  Capitalized terms used, but not otherwise defined, herein shall have their respective meanings set forth in the Exclusivity Agreement. 

W I T N E S S E T H 

WHEREAS, Disney and CAI are parties to that certain Exclusivity and Prepayment Agreement, dated as of the Effective Date (the “Exclusivity Agreement”); 

WHEREAS, following the execution of the Exclusivity Agreement by CAI and Disney, and Disney’s hiring of certain employees of CAI, CAI will need Disney to provide certain services, subject to the terms and conditions set forth herein, to facilitate CAI’s continued operation of its business and the orderly wind down of CAI’s Character.AI App, Website and Platform Services; 

WHEREAS, Disney may provide, or cause its Group members or Sub-Contractors to provide, to CAI such services for a limited period of time from and after the Effective Date; 

WHEREAS, Disney and CAI entered into that certain Transition Services Agreement (the “Original Agreement”), dated as of the Effective Date; and 

WHEREAS, each of Disney and CAI desires to amend and restate the Original Agreement in its entirety to reflect herein the terms of their agreement with respect to such services, including to govern the Processing of Personal Data (as such terms are defined in <u>Schedule D) in connection with the provision and receipt of the Services, such that this</u> Agreement shall supersede and replace the Original Agreement in its entirety. 

NOW, THEREFORE, subject to the terms, conditions, covenants and provisions of this Agreement, for good and valuable consideration, the receipt and sufficiency of which are hereby acknowledged by this Agreement, Disney and CAI, for themselves, their successors and assigns, mutually covenant and agree as follows: 

# **ARTICLE I** 

# **DEFINITIONS** 

- 1.1 <u>Definitions.  As used in this Agreement, the following terms shall have the</u> 

- following meanings: 

“Affected Party” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 8.1. 

“Affiliate” means, with respect to a specified Person, any other Person that, directly or indirectly through one or more intermediaries, controls, is controlled by or is under common

control with, such specified Person.  As used in this Agreement, the term “control” (including, with correlative meanings, the terms “controlled by” and “under common control with” and whether or not such terms are capitalized), as used with respect to any Person, means the possession, directly or indirectly, of the power to direct or cause the direction of the management and policies of such Person, whether through ownership of voting securities, by contract or otherwise. 

“Agreement” has the meaning ascribed thereto in the preamble. 

“Applicable Termination Date” means, with respect to each Service, the termination date specified with respect to such Service in Schedule A, which in each case, shall occur no later than the five (5)-month anniversary of the Effective Date. 

“CAI” has the meaning ascribed thereto in the preamble. 

“CAI Group” means, collectively, CAI and its Subsidiaries. 

“CAI Group Parties” means, collectively, the members of the CAI Group and their respective Representatives. 

“Cash Call” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 3.3. 

- “Confidential Information” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 7.1. 

“Deliverables” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(p). 

   - “Disney” has the meaning ascribed thereto in the preamble. 

   - “Disney Group” means Disney and its Affiliates. 

- “Disney Group Parties” means, collectively, the members of the Disney Group and their 

- respective Representatives. 

   - “Dispute” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(d). 

   - “Dispute Notice” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(d). 

   - “Effective Date” means September 18, 2026. 

   - “Excluded Service” means any service identified in Schedule B. 

   - “Exclusivity Agreement” has the meaning ascribed thereto in the recitals. 

   - “Executive Leadership Group” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(c). 

   - “Force Majeure Event” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 8.1. 

“Governmental Authority” means any transnational, domestic or foreign federal, state or local governmental, regulatory or administrative authority, department, court of competent

jurisdiction, arbitral body (public or private), agency, commission or official, including any political subdivision thereof. 

“Group” means either the Disney Group or the CAI Group, as the context requires. 

“Law” means, with respect to any Person, any transnational, domestic or foreign federal, state or local law (statutory, common or otherwise), constitution, treaty, convention, ordinance, code, rule, regulation, permit, order, injunction, judgment, decree, ruling or other similar requirement enacted, adopted, issued, promulgated, enforced or applied by a Governmental Authority that is binding upon or applicable to such Person, as amended unless expressly specified otherwise. 

“Omitted Services” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.2. 

“Original Agreement” has the meaning ascribed thereto in the recitals. 

“Party” means either CAI or Disney, and “Parties” means both CAI and Disney. 

“Performing Party” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 8.1. 

“Person” means an individual, firm, corporation, partnership, limited liability company, association, trust, joint venture or other entity or organization, including a Governmental Authority. 

“Qualified Bidder Diligence Services” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.4. 

“Representatives” means, with respect to any Person, its directors, members, partners, officers, employees, agents and advisors (including legal, financial and accounting advisors). 

“Sales Taxes” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 3.1(b). 

“Service Employee” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(e). 

“Service Manager” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(c). 

“Service Provider” means one or more members of the Disney Group in its or their capacity as the provider(s) of any Services to any Service Recipient. 

“Service Provider Data” means (a) any data or information of the Service Provider or its Group members, or their respective vendors, customers or other business partners, that is provided to or obtained by the Service Recipient or its personnel in the performance of the Service Recipient’s obligations under this Agreement and (b) any data or information to the extent related to the Service Provider or its Group members that is collected, generated or processed by the Service Recipient or its personnel solely in connection with the performance of the Service Recipient’s obligations pursuant to this Agreement. 

“Service Recipient” means one or more members of the CAI Group in its or their capacity as the recipient(s) of any Services from any Service Provider.

“Service Recipient Data” means (a) any data or information of the Service Recipient or its Group members, or their respective vendors, customers or other business partners, that is provided to or obtained by the Service Provider or its personnel in the performance of the Service Provider’s obligations under this Agreement and (b) any data or information to the extent related to the Service Recipient or its Group members that is collected, generated or processed by the Service Provider or its personnel solely in connection with the performance of the Service Provider’s obligations pursuant to this Agreement. 

“Services” means the individual services identified in Schedule A. 

“Sub-Contractor” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(f). 

“Taxes” means all federal, state, county, provincial, national, local, foreign and other taxes, charges, fees, duties (including customs duties), levies or other assessments, including income, gross receipts, net proceeds, ad valorem, turnover, real and personal property (tangible and intangible), sales, use, goods and services, franchise, excise, value added, stamp, leasing, lease, user, transfer, fuel, occupational, interest equalization, license, payroll, environmental, capital stock, disability, severance, employee’s income withholding, other withholding, employment insurance and social security taxes, premiums or levies, which are imposed by any Governmental Authority, including any interest, penalties or additions to tax attributable thereto. 

“Third-Party Costs” has the meaning ascribed thereto in Section 2.1(f). 

# **ARTICLE II** 

# **SERVICES** 

# 2.1 <u>Provision of Services.</u> 

(a) Disney shall provide, or cause the applicable Service Provider or Sub-Contractor to provide, the Services to CAI and the applicable members of the CAI Group, in each case, as Service Recipient(s), in accordance with the terms of this Agreement.  The Parties acknowledge and agree that the Services are provided for the benefit of the operation of CAI’s business that exists as of the Effective Date on a transitional basis only.  Accordingly, as promptly as practicable following the execution of this Agreement, CAI agrees to use commercially reasonable efforts to cease using the Services or transition each Service to CAI’s or its Group members’ own internal organization or to alternate service providers under other arrangements. Service Recipient acknowledges that the Services are contingent on the Service Recipient Dependencies set forth on Schedule A.  The Service Provider shall use commercially reasonable efforts (taking into account the express limitations set forth in this Agreement, including in <u>Section 2.1(e)) to perform, or cause to be performed, the Services in substantially the same</u> manner, and with substantially the same degree of care, quality and priority, as such Services (or reasonably similar services) were provided by or on behalf of CAI to CAI’s business immediately prior to the Effective Date.  The Service Recipient agrees and acknowledges that the Services shall be provided by the Service Provider solely using Service Recipient Systems (as defined in Schedule D).  To the extent that Service Recipient Systems are not suitable for providing the Services or the Service Provider is unable to use or access Service Recipient

Systems, other than due to the fault of the Service Provider, the Service Provider shall be relieved and excused from any further obligation to provide or make available the applicable Services that could not be provided for such reason, and the Service Provider shall not be considered in breach of this Agreement for failure to provide such Services. 

(b) CAI shall pay, perform, discharge and satisfy, or cause the applicable members of the CAI Group to pay, perform, discharge and satisfy, as and when due, its and their respective obligations as Service Recipients under this Agreement in accordance with the terms of this Agreement. 

(c) Each of Disney and CAI agrees to appoint (i) an employee representative, which may be an employee of one of its Group members (each such representative, a “Service <u>Manager”), who shall have overall responsibility for overseeing the day-to-day operation,</u> compliance and performance of this Agreement and implementing, managing and coordinating the Services and (ii) an executive steering leadership group (each such group, an “Executive <u>Leadership Group”) that shall have responsibility for project oversight, including information</u> security standards, governance and dispute escalation, in each case, pursuant to this Agreement on behalf of Disney and CAI, respectively.  Initially, the Service Managers and Executive Leadership Groups shall be the individuals set forth in Schedule C.  Either Party may change its designated Service Manager or Executive Leadership Group at any time upon written notice given to the other Party in accordance with Section 8.7.  The Service Managers shall consult and coordinate with each other on a regular basis during the term of this Agreement. 

(d) Except as otherwise provided in this Agreement, the Parties shall resolve all disputes arising under or in connection with this Agreement (each, a “Dispute”) in accordance with the following procedures (including, for the avoidance of doubt, any Dispute relating to payments with respect to the Services).  All Disputes shall be first discussed in person, by teleconference or by video conference by the Service Managers within ten (10) calendar days after receipt of written notice from either Party specifying the nature of the Dispute and requesting that the resolution process set forth in this Section 2.1(d) be initiated (a “Dispute <u>Notice”).  If any Dispute is not resolved by the Service Managers within fourteen (14) calendar</u> days after receipt of a Dispute Notice, or such longer period as the Service Managers may agree in writing, then, upon the written request of either Party, the Executive Leadership Groups shall meet in person, by teleconference or by video conference for the purpose of resolving the Dispute.  The Executive Leadership Groups shall negotiate in good faith to resolve the Dispute. If the Executive Leadership Groups do not resolve the Dispute within fourteen (14) calendar days after the date the Dispute was referred to them, or such longer period as the Executive Leadership Groups may agree in writing, the Parties may pursue any other rights, remedies or actions that may be available to them in accordance with Section 8.4. 

(e) The Service Provider shall use commercially reasonable efforts to cause the Services to be performed by the individual(s) who have historically performed such Services (or reasonably similar services) during the twelve (12) months prior to the Effective Date (each such individual, a “Service Employee”), in each case only for so long as such Service Employee(s) remain employed by the Service Provider (or its Group member or Sub-Contractor, as applicable) and is reasonably available to perform the applicable Service.  In the event that any Service Employee(s) are not (or are no longer so) employed by the Service Provider, the Service

Provider shall use commercially reasonable efforts to assign, and make available, personnel familiar with the applicable Service (solely to the extent such personnel exist) to perform such Service; provided, <u>that, if there are no other personnel familiar with the applicable Service, then</u> the Service Provider shall be relieved and excused from any further obligation to provide or make available the Services historically performed by such Service Employee(s), and the Service Provider shall not be considered in breach of this Agreement for failure to provide such Services. Subject to the foregoing, the Service Provider shall determine (in its sole and absolute discretion) the personnel who shall perform the Services to be provided by the Service Provider.  For the avoidance of doubt, the Service Provider shall not be obligated to cause the Services to be performed by any individual(s), including independent contractors, other than the Service Employees and other personnel familiar with the applicable Services, including any legacy employees, independent contractors or representatives of the Service Provider.  The Service Recipient acknowledges and agrees that no such personnel shall be required to dedicate more than twenty percent (20%) of their hours of employment by or work on behalf of the Service Provider to the performance or support of the Services.  All personnel performing Services shall remain at all times, and be deemed to be, employees, independent contractors or representatives solely of the Service Provider responsible for providing such Services (or of such Service Provider’s Group member or Sub-Contractor, as the case may be) for all purposes, and shall not be, or be deemed to be, employees, independent contractors or representatives of the Service Recipient.  Service Recipient also shall not be considered a joint employer of any of the employees, independent contractors, or representatives performing the Services.  The Service Provider (or its Group member or Sub-Contractor, as the case may be) shall be solely responsible for (x) payment of (i) all compensation, (ii) all income, disability, withholding and other employment Taxes and (iii) all medical benefit premiums, vacation pay, sick pay and other employee benefits payable to or with respect to personnel who perform Services on behalf of the Service Provider, as well as (y) (i) the filing, collection, withholding, and transmission of all federal, state and local income, unemployment, payroll and employment taxes and other withholdings required by federal, state or local law or regulation payable with respect to the employees providing the Services in accordance with applicable law, and (ii) providing unemployment insurance and workers’ compensation benefits to all such employees.  In addition, Service Provider shall be responsible for ensuring that such employees comply with all written policies and legal requirements applicable to the performance of the Services.  All such personnel shall be under the sole direction, control and supervision of the Service Provider (or its Group member or Sub-Contractor, as the case may be) and the Service Provider (or its Group member or Sub-Contractor, as the case may be) has the sole right to exercise all authority with respect to the employment, substitution, termination, assignment and compensation of such personnel. 

(f) The Service Provider may, at its option, from time to time, (i) delegate any or all of its obligations to perform Services under this Agreement to any one or more of its Group members or (ii) engage the services of other professionals, consultants or other third parties (each, a “Sub-Contractor”) in connection with the performance of the Services; provided, <u>however, that the Service Provider shall remain ultimately responsible for ensuring that its</u> obligations hereunder are satisfied with respect to any Services provided by any such Group member or Sub-Contractor and shall be liable for any such Group member’s or Sub-Contractor’s failure to so satisfy such obligations; provided, <u>further, that Disney’s Service Manager, and not</u> any such Group member or Sub-Contractor, shall be the Service Recipient’s sole point of contact

regarding the Services, including with respect to payment.  CAI shall reimburse Disney for all documented costs, fees and expenses paid by Disney or the applicable Service Provider to any third party, including Sub-Contractors, in connection with the provision of the Services (“Third- <u>Party Costs”), including any documented third-party fees, penalties or other costs incurred in</u> connection with third-party consents, waivers or licenses that Disney or the applicable Service Provider reasonably determines are necessary for the provision of the Services. 

(g) The Services shall be used by the applicable Service Recipient solely for the operation of CAI’s business for substantially the same purpose and in substantially the same manner as used by the CAI business during the three (3) months prior to the Effective Date, and for the orderly wind down of CAI’s Character.AI App, Website and Platform Services. 

(h) In the event that the Service Provider reasonably deems it necessary to suspend delivery of a Service hereunder for maintenance, repair or replacement or legal, compliance or security purposes, the Service Provider shall not be obligated to deliver such Service during such periods, but shall use commercially reasonable efforts to minimize the duration and impact of the interruption.  Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement, in no event shall the Service Provider (or any of its Group members or Sub-Contractors) be required to (i) provide Services to any Person that the Service Recipient directly or indirectly acquires after the Effective Date, or in any jurisdiction in which Services were not provided as of the Effective Date, (ii) provide Services to the extent that the provision of any such Service would require any consent, waiver or license of any third party, unless and until such consent, waiver or license has been obtained, (iii) expand its facilities, incur any capital expenditures, employ additional personnel or maintain the employment of any specific persons to provide the Services hereunder or (iv) disclose to any member of the CAI Group any contracts by which any member of the Disney Group acquires from third parties any components or inputs to any Service. 

(i) The Service Recipient shall cooperate with the Service Provider to the extent necessary or appropriate to facilitate the performance of the Services in accordance with the terms of this Agreement.  Without limiting the generality of the foregoing, (i) each Party shall, and shall cause its Group members to, give the other Party and its Group members and, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors reasonable access to, and necessary rights to use, any information, assets, systems, materials and technologies to the extent reasonably necessary for the performance or receipt of the Services and (ii) the Service Recipient shall, and shall cause its Group members to, upon reasonable written notice, give to the Service Provider and its Group members and Sub-Contractors reasonable access, during regular business hours and at such other times as are reasonably required, to the relevant premises and personnel to the extent reasonably necessary for the performance of the Services; provided, that Disney shall only be required to grant such access and rights to (1) legacy employees of the CAI business and (2) such other employees of the CAI Group that Disney, in its sole discretion, has approved in writing.  Each Party shall (and shall cause its Group members and their personnel and, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors’ personnel, providing or receiving Services to): (A) not attempt to obtain access to, use or interfere with (1) any information technology systems of the other Party or its Group members, or (2) any confidential or competitively sensitive information owned, used or processed by the other Party, except, in each case, to the extent reasonably necessary to do so to provide or receive Services or as permitted under Section 2.1(i)(i); (B) maintain reasonable security measures to protect the systems of the other Party and its Group members to which it

has access pursuant to this Agreement against access by unauthorized third parties; (C) not disable, damage or erase or disrupt or impair the normal operation of the information technology systems of the other Party or its Group members; and (D) promptly notify the other Party of any confirmed misuse, disclosure or loss of, or inability to account for, the other Party’s confidential or competitively sensitive information, and any confirmed unauthorized access to its or any member of its Group’s facilities, systems or network reasonably likely to affect the other Party and shall investigate such confirmed security incidents and reasonably cooperate with the other Party’s incident response team to mitigate and limit the impact of such a security incident. 

(j) CAI acknowledges that Disney is agreeing to perform, or cause to be performed, the Services in order to facilitate the release of Released Matters (as defined in the Exclusivity Agreement) set forth in Section 4 ( _Release and Waiver_ ) of the Exclusivity Agreement and the operation of the CAI business, and support the orderly wind down of CAI’s Character.AI App, Website and Platform Services.  The Services shall not include, and no Service Provider (or any Group member or Sub-Contractor of a Service Provider) shall be obligated to provide, any Service the provision of which to a Service Recipient from and after the Effective Date would constitute, or reasonably be expected to constitute, (i) a violation of any Law or (ii) a breach of any contractual obligation owed to a third party. 

(k) Each Party acknowledges that it may be necessary for each Party to make proprietary and/or third-party software available to the other Party in the course and for the purpose of performing or receiving, as applicable, Services.  Each Party (i) shall, and shall cause the members of its Group and, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors to, comply with the license restrictions applicable to any and all proprietary or third-party software made available to such Party by the other Party in the course of the provision of Services hereunder, (ii) acknowledges the license terms of use applicable to all proprietary or third party software in its possession as of the Effective Date and (iii) agrees that it shall be responsible for providing to the other Party a copy of the applicable license terms (or, solely with respect to open source software or other software with publicly available license terms, information sufficient to direct such other Party to a copy thereof) for any and all proprietary or third-party software first made available to such other Party from and after the Effective Date, solely to the extent such provision would not violate the providing Party’s duty of confidentiality owed to any third party.  In the event there are any additional licensing or other costs associated with the provision of third party software pursuant to this Section 2.1(k), such costs shall be borne by the Service Recipient. 

(l) Subject to Section 2.2, the Parties agree that the Services constitute all of the services to be provided to the CAI Group by Disney (or its Group members or Sub-Contractors, as the case may be) from and after the Effective Date. 

(m) CAI and its Group members shall not be granted or have access to Disney computer systems.  If there is a need for such access, CAI and Disney will agree on the terms pursuant to which such access will be allowed. 

(n) Each Party agrees to comply with the data processing addendum attached to and incorporated into this Agreement as set forth in Schedule D, which shall govern the Processing of Personal Data (as such terms are defined in Schedule D) in connection with the provision and receipt of the Services.  The Parties shall, as reasonably requested by the other Party, execute all

further documents as are reasonably necessary to comply with applicable Data Protection Laws (as defined in Schedule D) in connection with the provision and receipt of the Services. 

(o) Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement, the Parties acknowledge and agree that, as between the Parties, any and all Service Recipient Data shall be owned by the Service Recipient or its applicable Group member, subject to any limitations imposed by third-party vendors or subcontractors with respect thereto, and any and all Service Provider Data shall be owned by the Service Provider or its applicable Group member, subject to any limitations imposed by third-party vendors or subcontractors with respect thereto. 

(p) Any work product, deliverables, documents, code, materials or other tangible or intangible output (other than Service Recipient Data and Service Provider Data, which are addressed above) created, developed or prepared by or on behalf of the Service Provider specifically for the Service Recipient in the course of performing the Services (collectively, “Deliverables”) shall be owned by the Service Recipient, and the Service Provider hereby assigns, and shall cause its Group members and Sub-Contractors to assign, to the Service Recipient all right, title and interest in and to such Deliverables, including any intellectual property rights therein or thereto. 

2.2 <u>Omitted Service Amendments.  From time to time during the forty-five (45)-day</u> period commencing on the Effective Date, CAI may request Disney to provide, or cause the applicable Service Provider or Sub-Contractor to provide, services (other than Excluded Services) that are not identified in Schedule A that (a) were provided, directly or indirectly, by the CAI Group to the CAI business during the three (3) months prior to the Effective Date, (b) are reasonably necessary for the continued operation of the CAI business for substantially the same purpose and in substantially the same manner as it was conducted during the three (3) months prior to the Effective Date or reasonably necessary for the orderly wind down of CAI’s Character.AI App, Website and Platform Services and (c) a Service Recipient cannot otherwise secure from a third party on commercially reasonable terms (“Omitted Services”).  In the event that CAI desires to have Disney provide, or cause the applicable Service Provider or SubContractor to provide, Omitted Services and Disney is reasonably capable of providing, or causing the applicable Service Provider or Sub-Contractor to provide, such Omitted Services, then CAI and Disney shall in good faith negotiate an amendment to Schedule A, which shall describe in detail the service, project scope, term, price and payment terms applicable to such Omitted Services.  Any request for an Omitted Service shall be in writing and shall specify (a) the type and the scope of the requested service, (b) where and to whom the requested service is to be provided and (c) the proposed term for the requested service; provided, that the term of any Omitted Service shall terminate no later than five (5) months following the Effective Date.  Once agreed to in writing, the amendment to Schedule A shall be deemed part of this Agreement as of such date and the Omitted Services shall be deemed “Services” provided hereunder, subject to the terms and conditions of this Agreement. 

2.3 <u>No Management Authority.  No Service Provider (or any Group member or Sub-</u> Contractor of a Service Provider) shall be authorized by, or shall have any responsibility under, this Agreement to manage the affairs of the business of any Service Recipient.

2.4 <u>Diligence Services.  Disney and CAI shall work together in good faith to identify</u> five (5) Key Employees (as defined in the Exclusivity Agreement) who shall be made reasonably available for diligence with potential bidders who (a) have signed a non-disclosure agreement with CAI, (b) have submitted evidence to CAI demonstrating the party’s financial capability to consummate a proposed transaction, (c) have submitted to CAI a preliminary indication of interest and (d) CAI expects will be deemed to be qualified bidders under the relevant bidding procedures approved by the U.S. Bankruptcy Court for the District of Delaware (such Services provided by the Key Employees, the “Qualified Bidder Diligence Services”). 

# **ARTICLE III** 

# **COMPENSATION** 

# 3.1 <u>Compensation for Services.</u> 

(a) The Parties have agreed that the Services Provider will not charge a fee to the Service Recipient with respect to each Service rendered pursuant to this Agreement.  However, the Service Recipient shall reimburse the Service Provider for any Third-Party Costs in accordance with the procedures set forth in Section 3.2. 

(b) The amount of any sales Tax, value-added Tax, goods and services Tax or similar Tax that is required to be assessed and remitted by any Service Provider in connection with the Services provided hereunder (“Sales Taxes”) shall be promptly paid to the Service Provider by the Service Recipient in accordance with Section 3.2.  Such payment shall be in addition to the Third-Party Costs (unless such Sales Tax is expressly already accounted for in the applicable Third-Party Costs). 

(c) The Parties shall, and shall cause each of their respective Group members to, cooperate with each other in good faith and provide any information and/or documentation reasonably requested from time to time, in each case to enable the other Party to handle any audits, examinations or inquiries with respect to Sales Taxes and to enable the other Party to comply with applicable Law with respect to Sales Taxes. 

# 3.2 <u>Payment Terms.</u> 

(a) The Service Provider shall bill the Service Recipient within sixty (60) days after the end of each full calendar month following the Effective Date, or at such other interval specified with respect to a particular Service in Schedule A, an amount equal to the Third-Party Costs due for all Services provided in such month or other specified interval, as applicable, plus any Sales Taxes; provided, that the Service Provider may, in its sole discretion, bill the Service Recipient more frequently for Third-Party Costs as and when the Service Provider receives bills therefor from the applicable third parties.  Invoices shall be directed to the Service Manager appointed by CAI or to such other Person designated in writing from time to time by such Service Manager.  The Service Recipient shall pay such amount in full within thirty (30) days after receipt of each invoice by wire transfer of immediately available funds to the account designated by the Service Provider for this purpose or upon the Service Provider’s written request, directly to the applicable third party.  If the last day of such time period is not a Business

Day, the Service Recipient shall pay such amount on or before the following Business Day.  In addition to any other remedies for non-payment, if any payment is not received by the Service Provider on or before the date such amount is due, then a late payment interest charge, calculated at a ten percent (10.0%) per annum rate, compounded monthly, shall immediately begin to accrue and any such late payment interest charges shall become immediately due and payable in addition to the amount otherwise owed under this Agreement and provided for in the applicable invoice. 

(b) If the Service Recipient objects to the amount of any invoice, the Service Recipient shall pay the full amount of the invoice, including any amounts to which the Service Recipient objects, to the Service Provider and shall promptly provide written notice to the Service Provider of any amounts billed and paid to which the Service Recipient objects, reasonably detailing the basis for the Service Recipient’s objection.  Any such objection shall be deemed to be a Dispute hereunder subject to the provisions applicable to Disputes set forth in <u>Section 2.1(d).</u> 

3.3 <u>Cash Calls.  If Disney expects that the Service Provider shall incur material Third-</u> Party Costs in connection with the provision of any Services pursuant to this Agreement, Disney may, at its option, provide a written cash call (“Cash Call”) to CAI of the amount of such ThirdParty Costs in advance of the Service Provider incurring the same and CAI shall within five (5) days of receipt of such Cash Call provide such amount to the Service Provider for payment by wire transfer of immediately available funds to the account designated by Disney for this purpose. 

3.4 <u>Books and Records.  Disney shall, and shall cause each Service Provider to,</u> maintain complete and accurate books of account and other documentation as reasonably necessary to support the accuracy of invoices issued pursuant to Section 3.2(a). 

# **ARTICLE IV** 

# **TERM** 

4.1 <u>Commencement.  This Agreement is effective as of the Effective Date and shall</u> remain in effect with respect to a particular Service until the occurrence of the Applicable Termination Date with respect to such Service, unless earlier terminated (a) in its entirety or with respect to a particular Service, in each case in accordance with Section 4.2, or (b) by mutual consent of the Parties. 

4.2 <u>Termination.  If a Service Provider or Service Recipient materially breaches any</u> of its respective obligations under this Agreement (and the period for resolution of the Dispute relating to such breach set forth in Section 2.1(d) has expired), the non-breaching Party may terminate this Agreement with respect to the Service for which such obligations are owed, effective upon not less than thirty (30) days’ written notice of termination to the breaching Party, if the breaching Party does not cure such breach within thirty (30) days after receiving written notice thereof from the non-breaching Party.  The termination of this Agreement with respect to any Service pursuant to this Section 4.2 shall not affect the Parties’ rights or obligations under

this Agreement with respect to any other Service; provided, that any other Service upon which the performance of such Service is dependent or bundled shall also be terminated. 

4.3 <u>Effect of Termination.  In the event of any termination of this Agreement in its</u> entirety or with respect to any Service, Service Provider shall have no further obligation to provide the applicable terminated Services, but each Party, Service Provider and Service Recipient shall remain liable for all of their respective obligations that accrued hereunder prior to the date of such termination, including all obligations of each Service Recipient to pay any amounts due to any Service Provider hereunder pursuant to Section 3.1, <u>Section 3.2 and Section 3.3.  In addition to the surviving obligations set forth in the preceding sentence, Section 2.1(n),</u> this Section 4.3, <u>Article V, Article VI, Section 7.1, Section 7.2 and Article VIII shall each</u> survive the termination of this Agreement. 

# **ARTICLE V** 

# **LIMITATION ON LIABILITY; DISCLAIMER**

(a) NOTWITHSTANDING ANYTHING HEREIN TO THE CONTRARY, THE CAI GROUP PARTIES ACKNOWLEDGE AND AGREE THAT THE SERVICES ARE BEING PROVIDED “AS IS” AND AS AN ACCOMMODATION AND WITHOUT SEPARATE CONSIDERATION.  ACCORDINGLY, EXCEPT TO THE EXTENT FINALLY DETERMINED BY A COURT OF COMPETENT JURISDICTION TO HAVE RESULTED FROM THE GROSS NEGLIGENCE, WILLFUL MISCONDUCT OR ACTUAL FRAUD OR BAD FAITH OF A DISNEY GROUP PARTY, OR ANY BREACH BY A DISNEY GROUP PARTY OF ITS OBLIGATIONS UNDER SECTION 7.1 (CONFIDENTIALITY) (AS TO WHICH NO LIMITATION UNDER THIS SENTENCE SHALL APPLY), NO DISNEY GROUP PARTY SHALL HAVE ANY LIABILITY TO ANY CAI GROUP PARTY ARISING OUT OF, RELATING TO OR IN CONNECTION WITH THIS AGREEMENT OR THE SERVICES PROVIDED HEREUNDER. 

(b) IN NO EVENT SHALL ANY DISNEY GROUP PARTY BE LIABLE, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, IN TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE AND STRICT LIABILITY) OR OTHERWISE TO ANY CAI GROUP PARTY FOR ANY INDIRECT, SPECIAL, CONSEQUENTIAL, INCIDENTAL OR PUNITIVE DAMAGES (INCLUDING LOSS OF PROFITS) AS A RESULT OF ANY BREACH, PERFORMANCE OR NONPERFORMANCE BY DISNEY OR ANY SERVICE PROVIDER OR SUB-CONTRACTOR UNDER THIS AGREEMENT.

THE SERVICES TO BE PROVIDED</u> UNDER THIS AGREEMENT ARE FURNISHED WITHOUT REPRESENTATION OR WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY WARRANTY OF MERCHANTABILITY OR FITNESS FOR ANY PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  NO DISNEY GROUP PARTY MAKES ANY REPRESENTATION OR WARRANTY THAT ANY SERVICE COMPLIES WITH ANY LAW, DOMESTIC OR FOREIGN.

# **ARTICLE VI** 

# **INDEMNIFICATION** 

6.1 <u>Indemnification.  The Service Recipient shall defend, indemnify and hold the</u> Service Provider and its Affiliates, and their respective officers, directors, shareholders, agents, employees, contractors and assigns of each, harmless from and against any and all suits, claims, demands, penalties, fines, charges, proceedings, causes of action, damages, losses, liabilities, costs and expenses of any nature whatsoever (including attorneys’ fees) arising directly or indirectly from or out of: (a) the conduct and operation of the CAI business (including CAI’s Character.AI App, Website and Platform Services) while the Services are provided under this Agreement, (b) the Service Recipient’s use of the Services or (c) the Service Recipient’s failure to comply with its obligations under this Agreement.  The provisions of this Section 6.1 shall survive any determination that this Agreement or any portion hereof is void or voidable. 

# **ARTICLE VII** 

# **OTHER COVENANTS** 

# 7.1 <u>Confidentiality.</u> 

(a) Each Party hereby acknowledges that confidential information of such Party or its Group members may be exposed to the other Party or its Group members, or its or their respective Representatives (including, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors), as a result of the activities contemplated by this Agreement (such information, “Confidential <u>Information”).  Each Party agrees that Confidential Information is a valuable asset of its owner,</u> and its public disclosure or use outside of the activities contemplated by this Agreement without the prior consent of the relevant Party or its Group member would cause substantial harm to such disclosing Party or other Person.  Therefore, each Party agrees to treat, and to cause its Group members and its and their respective Representatives (including, in the case of Disney, its SubContractors) to treat, all Confidential Information received by it with the amount of care that a reasonable business Person would use to protect its own valuable and proprietary Confidential Information and not to disclose any Confidential Information to any Person who does not have a contractual or fiduciary obligation with such Person to keep such Confidential Information confidential.  In addition, neither Party shall utilize or permit its Group members, or its or their respective Representatives (including, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors), to utilize Confidential Information for any purpose other than for the benefit of the CAI or the owner of such information, as the case may be. 

(b) Notwithstanding the foregoing, each Party may disclose Confidential Information to its Group members and its and their respective Representatives (including, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors), on a need-to-know basis; provided that such Party shall remain liable for any breach of this Section 7.1 by its Group members and its and their respective Representatives (including, in the case of Disney, its Sub-Contractors). 

(c) Except as may be required by Law or stock exchange rule, neither Party shall, without the prior written consent of the other Party, advertise, issue any press release or

otherwise publicize the fact that the Parties have entered into this Agreement or disclose the terms and conditions of this Agreement; provided, that CAI may disclose the existence of this Agreement and its general scope and terms (i) in connection with CAI’s chapter 11 bankruptcy filing and related proceedings, including in any filings with, or disclosures to, the Bankruptcy Court, and (ii) to any official committee of unsecured creditors appointed in CAI’s chapter 11 case, or its professional advisors, which disclosure under this clause (ii) may be made on a confidential, professionals’-eyes-only or similarly restricted basis. 

(d) As used herein, “Confidential Information” shall not include information (i) that has become generally available to the public through no fault of the receiving Person, (ii) either (x) is known to the receiving Person prior to being furnished by the disclosing Person, or (y) becomes available to the receiving Person on a non-confidential basis from a source other than the disclosing Person; provided that the source was not prohibited by any legal, contractual or fiduciary obligation to the disclosing Person or any third party from disclosing such information, or (iii) disclosed to the extent necessary pursuant to (1) any Law, (2) the order of a court of competent jurisdiction, (3) any rule or regulation of an administrative agency, (4) any stock exchange rule or other market or reporting system, (5) any deposition, interrogatory, oral questions, request for information or documents, subpoena, civil investigative demand or similar process, (6) any required report, statement or testimony submitted to any municipal, state or national (including foreign regulatory) bodies having or claiming to have jurisdiction, or (7) other legal, judicial, arbitration, regulatory or administrative process or proceeding; provided that, under the circumstances described in this subparagraph (iii), the Person required to make the disclosure shall, to the extent legally permissible, notify in writing the Person whose Confidential Information shall be disclosed of such event and shall cooperate with any reasonable request or efforts by such Person, at such Person’s sole expense, to take reasonable legally permissible actions to limit the scope of disclosure required in connection therewith and shall exercise reasonable efforts to obtain assurance that confidential treatment shall be accorded to such Confidential Information. 

(e) The terms of this Section 7.1 shall continue to apply after the expiration or termination of this Agreement for (i) two (2) years with respect to Confidential Information concerning (A) the intellectual property rights of a Party or its Group members, (B) clients, vendors and customers of a Party or its Group members and (C) the business plans of a Party or its Group members and (ii) one (1) year with respect to all other Confidential Information. 

7.2 <u>Use of Disney Name.  CAI shall acquire no right under this Agreement to use,</u> and CAI shall not use, the name “Disney”, “ABC”, “ESPN”, “Lucasfilm”, “Marvel” or “Pixar” (either alone or in conjunction with or as a part of any other word, mark or name) or any marks, fanciful characters or designs of Disney or any of its Group members (including, for purposes of clarity and the avoidance of doubt, The Walt Disney Company) in any manner whatsoever. 

7.3 <u>Exclusivity Agreement.  Each party hereto hereby agrees to perform under,</u> comply with and implement the terms of, the Exclusivity Agreement, which is attached hereto as <u>Exhibit A and incorporated into this Agreement.</u>

# **ARTICLE VIII** 

# **MISCELLANEOUS** 

8.1 <u>Force Majeure.  In case performance of any terms or provisions hereof shall be</u> delayed or prevented, in whole or in part, because of or related to compliance with any Law or requirement of any national securities exchange, or because of riot, war, public disturbance, pandemic (including any lockdowns, quarantines, “shelter in place” or “stay at home” orders, shutdowns, closures, sequesters and other protective measures in response thereto by any Governmental Authority), strike, labor dispute, unavailability of labor, fire, explosion, storm, flood, act of God or act of terrorism that is not within the control of the performing Party, Service Provider or Service Recipient whose performance is interfered with (each, a “Performing <u>Party”) and which by the exercise of reasonable diligence such Performing Party is unable to</u> prevent, or for any other reason which is not within the control of such Performing Party whose performance is interfered with and which by the exercise of reasonable diligence such Performing Party is unable to prevent (each, a “Force Majeure Event”), then upon prompt written notice, stating the date and extent of such interference and the Force Majeure Event which is the cause thereof, by the Performing Party to the other Party, Service Recipient or Service Provider (each, an “Affected Party”), as applicable, the Performing Party shall be excused from its obligations hereunder during the period such Force Majeure Event or its effects continue, and no liability shall attach against either the Performing Party or the Affected Party on account thereof; <u>provided, however, that the Performing Party shall promptly resume the required performance</u> upon the cessation of the Force Majeure Event or its effects.  No Performing Party shall be excused from performance under this Section 8.1 if such Performing Party fails to use commercially reasonable efforts to mitigate the effects of the Force Majeure Event on such Performing Party’s ability to perform hereunder. 

8.2 <u>Relationship of Parties.  Nothing in this Agreement shall be deemed or construed</u> by the Parties or any third party as creating a relationship of principal and agent, partnership or joint venture between the Parties, between any Service Provider and any Service Recipient or with any individual providing Services, it being understood and agreed that no provision contained herein, and no act of any Party or its Group members, shall be deemed to create any relationship between the Parties or Group members other than the relationship set forth herein. Each Party and each Service Provider shall act under this Agreement solely as an independent contractor and not as an agent or employee of the other Party or any of such Party’s Group members. 

# 8.3 <u>Counterparts; Effectiveness; Entire Agreement.</u> 

(a) This Agreement may be executed in counterparts, all of which shall be considered one and the same agreement and shall become effective when counterparts have been signed by each of the Parties and delivered to the other Party, it being understood that each Party need not sign the same counterpart.  Delivery of a copy of this Agreement bearing a Party’s signature by electronic mail in PDF format or by other similar electronic means shall have the same effect as physical delivery of the paper document bearing the original signature.

(b) This Agreement, the other Transaction Documents and the other agreements referred to herein and therein constitute the entire agreement of the Parties with respect to the subject matter hereof and thereof and supersede all prior agreements and undertakings, both written and oral, among the Parties with respect to the subject matter hereof and thereof. 

8.4 <u>Governing Law; Dispute Resolution.  This Agreement, and all claims or causes of</u> action whether in contract or tort, that may be based on or arise out of or relate to this Agreement, shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the Laws of the State of Delaware (without regard to any applicable principles of conflicts of law).  Each party hereto (a) irrevocably submits to the personal jurisdiction of the United States District Court for the District of Delaware, or if and only if the United States District Court does not have jurisdiction, then before the Court of Chancery of the State of Delaware, or if and only if neither such court shall have jurisdiction, then before any other state or federal court sitting in Delaware having subject matter jurisdiction, and (b) agrees that any actions or proceedings arising in connection with this Agreement shall be brought, tried and determined exclusively in the courts referred to in clause (a) of this Section 8.4 and consents to service of process outside the State of Delaware pursuant to the requirements of such court in any matter subject to it.  The parties irrevocably waive, to the fullest extent permitted by law, any objection that they may have to the laying of venue of any such action or proceeding in such courts and any claim that any such action or proceeding brought in any such court has been brought in an inconvenient forum. 

8.5 <u>Assignment.  This Agreement and the rights and obligations hereunder shall not</u> be assignable by either Party without the express written consent of the other Party; provided, that, without such consent, Disney may assign its rights and obligations hereunder to an Affiliate at any time.  Any attempted assignment in violation of this Section 8.5 shall be void.  Subject to the preceding sentences, this Agreement shall be binding upon, inure to the benefit of, and be enforceable by, the Parties and their respective successors and permitted assigns.  Nothing in this <u>Section 8.5 shall affect or impair (a) Disney’s ability to designate one or more Group members to</u> provide Services as a Service Provider hereunder, or a Service Provider’s ability to delegate any or all of its obligations under this Agreement to one or more Group members or Sub-Contractors pursuant to Section 2.1(f), or (b) CAI’s ability to designate one or more Group members to receive Services as a Service Recipient hereunder. 

8.6 <u>No Third Party Beneficiaries.  The agreements, covenants and representations</u> contained herein are for the benefit of the Parties and are not for the benefit of any third parties, except to the extent that any other Person is expressly granted any rights (including provisions applicable to a Party’s Group members and as contemplated by Article V) under this Agreement.

If any provision, sentence, phrase or word of this Agreement or the</u> application thereof to any Person or circumstance shall be held invalid, the remainder of this Agreement, or the application of such provision, sentence, phrase or word to Persons or circumstances other than those as to which it is held invalid, shall not be affected thereby and a suitable and equitable provision shall be substituted therefor in order to carry out, so far as may be valid and enforceable, the intent and purpose of such invalid provision. 

8.9 <u>Amendments and Waivers.  This Agreement can be amended, supplemented or</u> changed, and any provision hereof can be waived, only by written instrument making specific reference to this Agreement signed by the Party against whom enforcement of any such amendment, supplement, modification or waiver is sought.  The waiver by any Party of a breach of any provision of this Agreement shall not operate or be construed as a further or continuing waiver of such breach or as a waiver of any other or subsequent breach.  No failure on the part of any Party to exercise, and no delay in exercising, any right, power or remedy hereunder shall operate as a waiver thereof, nor shall any single or partial exercise of such right, power or remedy by such Party preclude any other or further exercise thereof or the exercise of any other right, power or remedy. 

8.10 <u>Representations and Warranties. # **Excluded Services** 

The following corporate services: 

1. legal and compliance advice; 

2. tax advice; 

3. human resources (including any hiring and firing decisions, or any actual hiring or firing of employees); 

4. employee benefits (including allocation or issue of employee credit cards and cell phones); 

5. treasury; 

6. public policy; 

7. government relations; 

8. communications and marketing; 

9. senior management; 

10. procurement; 

11. corporate development or strategy; and 

12. any disaster recovery, crisis management and/or business continuity arrangements.
